# MU-RECURSION task list

Derived from `MU-RECURSION.md` chapter 41 (landing order) and the audits of
2026-09-05. One task per commit; the commit message carries the task id, the
test names, and the `test result` line of one foreground
`cargo test --release`. The auditor reproduces the cold-boot trace before
the next task lands. Status as of the custody handover of 2026-09-07: the
carrier surgery was inherited in flight and uncommitted on `aa959c4`
(`basis` and `pending` are `Keyed`, `book_index`/`basis_index` deleted) and
red at 357/3. The G-set closes it: G1–G5 are done and the tree
is green at 365 lib, 13 alpha_plan, 6 g_cga, 1 bins under `-D warnings`.
G6 is a ruling, not a patch. Then the split (Stage 8), then the Cut B+1
soak rows C1–C3, which gate any word being ruled.

**The standing reading of this phase.** Five of the G-set's items changed
behaviour on paths **no test touched** — who an escrow pays, what a torsion
is worth, which word a chart condenses, what a held loop germinates from,
which word a slot names. The suite was byte-identical across three of them.
Under the beta's distributed premise this is the load-bearing risk: a
choice by list position is deterministic on one laptop and divergent across
a mesh, so the same mathematics ruled on two continents produces two
languages, and nothing in the suite would say so. Every remaining sweep row
carries "and a test per site" for that reason.

Legend: **done** verified on the tree; **partial** landed with a named gap;
**open** not started. "DoD" is the definition of done. "Rule" cites the
coding-standards rule most likely to be broken while doing the task.

| 0.7 | **partial — A12 landed as `transducer/factor.rs`, wired into the client's connection arm.** `core::zlinalg` gained `hnf_with_transform` (Hermite normal form over ℤ **with the unimodular transform**, exact over `i128`, refusing on overflow) and `reduce_against`. The transform is the point: an HNF without `U` decides membership and cannot say what the word was made of, and that is the whole question. `factor_on_plane` builds the even sublattice per step 1 — the even words, the differences of odd pairs, and `4·e_u` for the six quarter turns, which belongs to no word and is spent by nobody — decides membership exactly, and pulls the reduction back through `U` to exponents over the words the mind holds. A failure returns the **canonical** HNF residue, unique for the lattice, so two minds holding the same words speak the same demand: that is what makes a remainder a coordinate and not a choice. `factor_off_plane` is the budgeted walk over A1 keys, and an exhausted budget is `Unmeasured`, never `Novel` — a refusal is not a demand. `replays` verifies every answer by re-derivation (step 4). `Epigenome::comprehend`'s connection arm factors through the WHOLE fiber instead of testing one held representative at a time (M6 step 3), which the pairwise transition test could not do at all: a word may be a product of several held words while matching none of them. **Remaining:** `reduce_excluding`'s kernel group path still calls `best_peel`; retiring it needs `NormalForm` to carry signed exponents, since A12 returns them and `Decomposed(Vec<usize>)` cannot express an inverse | 16.12, 17.6 | core/zlinalg.rs; transducer/factor.rs; sdk/epigenome.rs; cognition/germinate.rs | **partial** | `a12_plane_factorisation_by_hnf`, `a12_plane_remainder_is_canonical`, `a12_offplane_descent_or_novel`, `m6_held_under_other_spelling_is_understood`, `a_factorisation_that_does_not_rederive_is_refused` |

## Whole-tree audit against the preamble (2026-09-06, at 75e26eb, non-test code)

Clean: no float or transcendental in cognition (the exact integer square root refuses when inexact); the digest gate is green and every key decodes; no `HashMap`/`BTreeMap` in `transducer/` or `server/`; `i < j` loops occur only over a simplex's own vertices (cech.rs) and inside fixed-dimension matrix arithmetic (core/engine.rs, torsion.rs); canonical orderings by key (`atlas_root`, syllabus by depth, class output by key) are orderings, not choices. Everything below is a site to close, keyed by rule; W-rows are the sweep.

| # | rule / law | site | what it does | write instead |

## Cut B+1 soak (recut 2026-09-06 21:51:35 on aa959c4): content bugs on a true carrier

The four gating commits verified (71524ef, 619f56f, 3a089c9, aa959c4; suite 358/13/6/1 reproduced; 5 matrix-, tile- and clock-era tests removed, 8 added). First claim holds on every voice: `form withheld` → `remainder expressed` → `dialect stood` → `uttered`, dialect 38 → 160 per voice in the first half hour. Everything after the utterance is choked, in this causal order:

| # | finding (evidence) | doctrine | write instead |
|---|---|---|---|
| C1 | **Room is drawn from the hypha's own bond, never the voice's, and never released.** `lane_room` reads `bond_of(&self.identity)` (the hypha's genesis bond `[8, 8, 1, 8]` × reserve/spend 8 = 64 per lane); `court room refused voice ce02…` names the hypha itself; draws of 70 on lane H exceed 64 on the first utterance, and room falls to 0 as occupancy accrues because nothing closes and nothing lapses (`court attest` 0 on hyphae a and c, ~10k refusals each) | 23.8 (the voice's room), rule 5 (the genesis bond and the ×8 are free constants unrelated to any chain's measure), 47.5 (lapse releases) | room is the uttering voice's: bond keyed by the voice's vk; the genesis bond is measured from the corpora at genesis (covers the largest single-pair μ⃗ the corpus presents, per lane), never a literal; a docket's draw is released on closure, ruling, or the 47.5 lapse (5.5), so the book cannot lock |
| C2 | **The percept path pends everything.** Hypha b answers 720 percepts with `valid: germ pending (one voice)`; `basis=0` after 30 min; `QUORUM = 2` requires a second *voice* on the same percept, which distinct tasks per voice never supply | 23.8 (a percept with a proof is replayed and ruled), 47.3 (the second organ is an independent witness hypha, never a second voice), W3 (`QUORUM` is a free constant) | a percept whose proof replays is sectioned at home and co-signed by a witness hypha outside the home's delegation closure; consilience by a second voice is a use, not a gate |
| C3 | **The syllabus teaches words no court ruled.** Hypha b logs 20 `court syllabus … to …` lines and voices show `genome 3–9` and `promoted` while every hypha has `basis=0` and zero installs; the taught words are the voices' own provisional dialect handed back as language | 23.8 (a taught word carries its ruling receipt; the receiver replays and refuses without one), 6 (the genome is exactly the ruled words) | `serve_ruled` serves the ruled cover only; U8's receiver replay requires the ruling receipt, not only a replaying tape; a provisional word on the syllabus is a refusal on both sides |
| C4 | **The T lane paces the voice into a stop.** `stroke idle: dwell [.., .., 909 → 4048, ..]` grows monotonically because the hypha's reported T pressure is the standing extent of listings that never close; the voice dwells thousands of strokes on a lane that is a cost, not a coordinate | law 9 (T is a cost, never a coordinate), U4 | the per-lane dwell reads the voice's own pressure on S, G, H; T never paces a stroke; a hypha's T pressure prices listings, it does not gate voices |
| C5 | **Nothing sheds, memory climbs.** `shed 0` on every stroke line (aperture unmeasured because no receipt was admitted, heights 0); held listings 285–329 and ideas 49–57 per voice; client RSS 84–121 MB and rising | 24.1, 25 (shed by measured aperture) | downstream of C1: once receipts are admitted the aperture is measured; until then a voice holds at most what its own reserve covers, refusing new held thoughts when the reserve is spent, never a count |
| C6 | **Hardening residues.** `genesis: [0u8; 32]` is a placeholder (`genesis 0000… epoch 2`); `Height::stamp` prints a height as `XX:XX:XX:XX:XX:XX`, a clock costume; `mint dd89… μ⃗ [8, 8, 1, 8] k 0` lines are genesis allocations, not words, and read as mints | 45.5, rule 11, 34.2 | the genesis hash is the hash of the genesis receipt bytes; a height prints as `h {n}`; allocations log `genesis voice … allocated`, never `mint` |
| C7 | **No pivot fires; no form closes on the mind.** `pivoted [0, 0, 0, 0]` on every stroke; `form spoken 0`; every family withheld with a remainder over all pairs; 8 of 40 `extract refused` | expected: depth-0 star forms have no loops, so A16 has nothing to discharge; closure needs glue and Mayer–Vietoris (2.3) and the refraction carrier (R1) | not a bug; the next set (R1, 2.3) is where closure comes from; `extract refused` reasons to be histogrammed |

## Gate audit (2026-09-07): every gate guilty until proven

Ruled: a gate nobody has watched fail is a costume. Each was probed by
writing a file that violates the rule it claims to enforce and checking the
build fails. Six fired on the obvious violation. Probing the shape the
DOCTRINE names rather than the shape each was written for found three gaps,
one of them a rule with no gate at all.

| gate | obvious violation | doctrine's shape | outcome |
|---|---|---|---|
| enumeration (`.entries()`, `.for_each(`, `spend_unit()`) | fires | a bare `for` over a `Vec` with no `Budget` (rule 10) is **silent** | open — not cheaply matchable; rule 10 stays a review rule, recorded here so it is not mistaken for gated |
| blake3 / digest (law 2, A21) | fires | fires through a `use` alias too | sound |
| clock (law 9, 45.6) | fires | `Instant::now`, `SystemTime::now` both fire | sound |
| matrix (law 11, rule 16) | fires | — | sound |
| file length (Stage 8) | fires on a 4371-line file | — | sound |
| squaring (law 11, checklist f′) | fires on `(Q, Q)` | **`placement: (i64, i64)` was silent** | **closed** — (f′) names `(i64, i64)` and the gate read only the rationals. It cannot match by type, since `(i64, i64)` IS the cell coordinate and is legitimate at the sensor boundary in `blade.rs` and `chain.rs`; it matches by ROLE, which is what (f′) says — a pair *carried as* a placement, shift or residue |
| maps (rule 9, checklist g) | — | `HashMap` in cognition was **silent** | **closed** — there was NO gate for this rule at all, and four transport tables had already landed in `client/sdk` (W7). `lint_maps` over `server`, `client`, `transducer`, `court`, with `network/` exempt as the sovereignty boundary, and the four tables converted to `Keyed` over the socket string's bytes as A10 prescribes |

One correction to my own probing: the first `(i64, i64)` widening matched by
type and fired on eleven legitimate cell-coordinate sites in `blade.rs` and
`chain.rs`. The probe was the costume, not the gate. Matching by role is
what (f′) actually states.

## Custody of the in-flight surgery (2026-09-07) — the G-set

The implementor node stopped mid-flight on the positional-carrier surgery.
The work is uncommitted on `aa959c4`, compiles, and is red at 357/3. It is
not stashed and not restarted: it is finished in place, its defects named in
the commit message rather than quietly repaired. Nothing else lands until
G2 is green.

Two claims of the W14 row are corrected here, so the next audit does not
re-derive them: `versors_close` **is** now projective (cognition.rs 177) —
the surgery fixed it, and the row is right about the present tree though not
about the tree it was written against; `best_peel` is **not** dead — it is
called from `decompose_over` at cognition.rs 4717 and is on the live
reduction path, so its retirement is 0.7's to perform, not a deletion of
unreachable code.

| id | task | rule / chapter | files | status | DoD |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| G1 | **done** — `precedes` measured two frontiers against one budget. chronometry.rs 370–371 passes the same `&mut Budget` to both `frontier` calls; the first walk drains it and the second returns empty, so every `kb` falls to `unwrap_or(0)`, `equal` stays true, and `precedes` answers `false` for receipts that do precede. It surfaces only where `spend_unit() = (reserve_max()/8).max(2)` floors near 2, so **the test's verdict depends on hardware calibration** — the claim T15 makes is about causal order, not about this box's reserve. Two faults: the shared budget, and `-> bool` making an exhausted walk indistinguishable from "does not precede" (rule 11: exact or refused). `precedes` also cloned the whole chain twice per call (354, 362), an O(chain) allocation on every `cocycle_at` supersession | 45.3, T15, rules 5, 11, 13 | chronometry.rs `precedes`, `frontier` | **done** — `frontier_from(head, budget)` walks from a receipt over the chain's own receipts, so no chain is copied to compare two of its members; `frontier` and `precedes` return `Option`, `None` when the budget cannot finish a walk; the tests pay `stroke_n(8)`, the size of the walks they reach | `precedes_implies_lower_height_and_concurrency_is_kept` (machine-independent), `a_frontier_the_budget_cannot_finish_is_refused_not_shortened` |
| G2 | **done** — the carrier surgery, and the genesis bond it exposed. Two faults, neither of them the `Keyed` conversion: (a) `basis()` returned `&self.language.words`, a snapshot that diverges from the carrier the moment `word_mut` calls `Arc::make_mut` (the refcount is 2, so the mutation lands in `basis` and the snapshot keeps the pre-puncture word) — the condense test read a punctured word back un-punctured. It reads the live carrier in key order now, documented as a display and test seam and never an index for cognition. (b) The diff also carried the first part of the Cut B+1 soak's C1, room per voice (`lane_room_of`, `drawn`, `genesis_cover`, `ensure_bonded`); `drawn` is released, symmetric add/subtract at 1191–1200, so the soak note's "never released" does not describe this tree. What broke was the bond it enrols at: `arc::family_cover` took a lane-wise **maximum** over a family's pairs where 12.6 requires the lane-wise **sum**, the pairs being disjoint scenes, so a genesis bond measured from a corpus was no larger than that corpus's largest single pair and could not cover a pair of it (`court room refused … draw 6 room 4`). `cover_join` accumulates and refuses on overflow; production already measures its cover from the registered corpus, so the six-family default in `Kernel::new` is a bootstrap starting corpus and is now named as one | rule 3, rule 12, rule 13, 12.6, law 2 | cognition.rs `basis`, `measured_test_cover`; arc.rs `cover_join`, `family_cover` | **done** | `family_cover_is_the_lane_wise_sum_of_its_pairs`; `the_condense_stroke_absorbs_a_family_into_one_bundle`; `independent_objects_fragment_and_resolve`; 361/13/6/1 under `-D warnings` |
| G3 | **done, and its primitive's test was hollow.** `verts × on_chart`, then an intersection by walking one trie and probing the other. The intersection of a cell's sections is `Keyed::meet` (A13, **already on the tree**, nerve.rs 415) folded over the cell's own vertex list — a boundary walk, not an enumeration (13.1, 14.6). The missing carrier is the transpose of `spoken` (`Keyed<listing → Keyed<voice>>`, 623): write `spoken_by: Keyed<voice → Keyed<listing>>` in `record_spoken` and the fold reads it. `volume_of_cell` and `pay_cell_volume` inherit the fix. The carrier and the fold were already on the tree, inherited with the surgery and correct: `spoken_by` is written beside `spoken` in `record_spoken` and removed symmetrically in `forget_spoken`, and `listings_on` folds `meet` over the cell's own vertices. What was missing was any claim — no test named either. Worse, the primitive underneath carried a test named after a theorem it did not exercise: `meet_is_intersection_and_costs_shared_prefixes` called `work::reset()` **before** building its tries, so the counter measured `n` inserts plus the meet, the N-to-4N ratio was the build's own linear growth, and the assertion `w4 <= w * 8` passed for any implementation that did not outright explode. Reset after construction, the real claim holds exactly: growing the larger trie's disjoint bulk 4× costs the meet **nothing**, equal counters. The implementation was sound; only the evidence was | 13.1, 14.6, A13, rule 1, rule 13 | cognition.rs `listings_on`; nerve.rs `meet` test | **done** | `listings_on_is_the_meet_of_its_vertices`; `meet_is_intersection_and_costs_shared_prefixes` asserting equality at N and 4N |
| G4a | **done — the settlement path paid by position.** Three sites, one claim: every party of a settlement is a party. `Treasury::release_rewrite` paid `voices.first().copied().unwrap_or(voice)` — an escrow accumulates its voices (`escrow_award` pushes each new one), so it paid one of several who stood the word, chosen by arrival order, with an invented fallback payee besides; it now pays every voice, split per lane as `settle_consilience` splits, and returns `None` rather than releasing an escrow with no party. `pay_cell_volume` paid `addrs.first()`, so a cell's volume went to whichever member it happened to list first; every vertex of the cell is now a party and the volume splits. `pay_torse` read **the magnitude** off `addrs.first()` too, so the value of a torsion depended on list order and was paid to that member alone; each member is now paid the torsion it measured, and a member with no measured tension is unmeasured and paid nothing rather than paid another member's reading. | G4b | **done — a choice by position is a consensus fork.** The distributed reading of rule 15, and the one that makes these urgent rather than untidy: chapter 12.6 requires two minds holding one loop to list the same candidates in the same order, so a member of a fiber picked by arrival order is a *different object on every node*, and identical mathematics yields two answers. Closed: `vs.last()` as "the failing versor" — there was no failing versor, `versors_close` composes the **whole** fiber and tests the product, so the obstruction is the holonomy (10.3) and the germ is bred from it; `versors_close` is now `fiber_holonomy(vs) -> Option<Versor>` with closure as `projective_eq` on the composite, and the singleton and empty cases are "not a loop" rather than "does not close". `torsion().last()` with `unwrap_or((Vec::new(), spanned))` — the least cycle by class coordinates, canonical; a word carrying no filed cycle has no torse to speak, since the fallback invented an empty sigma and a period the span stood in for that A17 never witnessed (T5). `cites.first()…unwrap_or_else(identity_versor)` — the transport a flat loop is recorded under is the **composite** of the cites by the geometric product, and no cites means no loop is recorded rather than one recorded under an invented identity. `stalks.fiber(&inv).first()` ×2 — the least address on the chart, so two hyphae condense the same word. `live_slot(..).unwrap_or(0)` ×3 — slot 0 is a real word and it is not the one meant; `basis_insert`/`basis_push`/`syntropic_on_admit` returned a slot **no caller read**, so the return is gone, and `Outcome::Sectioned` refuses when the snapshot lags rather than naming word 0. Verified not faults: `keys.last()` is a cursor resume (law 3 permits a `Keyed::cursor`); `settlements.first()` is a FIFO drain head | rules 4, 11, 15; 10.3, 12.6 alignment | cognition.rs `fiber_holonomy`, `torse_at`, `record_flat`, `optimize`, `basis_insert` | **done** | `a_held_fiber_germinates_its_holonomy_not_a_member` |

| G5 | **`same_base` duplicates an index that exists.** cognition.rs's `.find` comparing `element::positions(..) == element::positions(&centered)` walks a chart fiber by content while `by_footprint: Keyed<Keyed<()>>` (554) is written in `ruled_admit` (1140) and already read at 4516 and 4962. The `min_by_key(|e| e.chart.key())` on a listing field selects one open chart where a field with several non-flat entries has several | rule 1, rule 4 | cognition.rs | **open** — with G4 | the footprint read is a `get`; the field returns its fiber |

| G6 | **done — ruled: it was never a refinement.** `try_refine` mints `RewritePayload::Refine { chart: inv, axis: addr[0] as u64 }`. Under A21 the first byte of an address is the **tag byte naming the object kind**, so every Refine rewrite of a given kind claims the same axis, and chapter 35's invariant — "Every chart axis is an exact invariant; no axis is a hash fragment" — is broken by a fragment of an address standing in for A7's class invariants (16.7: the axis comes from the class rotor, never from the bodies that carried it). Beside it, `after` is `self.language.words.first()…unwrap_or_else(candidate.clone())`: the rewrite's result is an arbitrary word of the language. **The diagnosis is that the payload is mislabelled**: this move retires a word whose meaning its cites reproduce, which is retirement by citation (3.8, chapter 27, T10), not refinement of a chart (3.7). There is no axis because no chart is being refined, which is why nothing honest could fill the field. **Ruled 2026-09-07: retirement by citation is its own move**, and it is the compression axis. `RewriteKind::Retire` with `RewritePayload::Retire { cites, freed }`: `cites` are the words that speak the retired one, `freed` is the lane vector the language stopped carrying. The yield is `freed` — the compression itself — where `Refine` paid a **constant** (`yield_from_payload` hardcoded 1 on the shell lane and `yield_vector` carried `.max(1)` so the two would agree, which is rule 5 wearing a measurement's clothes). The court checks from the tape what the tape can prove: a retirement that cites nothing is a deletion, one that cites the word it retires proves nothing, and one naming another vertex as `after` claims a change of meaning it did not make — the result does not move, only the structure (3.8, T10). That the cites genuinely reproduce the retired substance is a re-derivation over the language and stays the kernel's, made by `verify_utterance` before the mint. ## Stage 8c — after the splits (audit 2026-09-07 at 3c02252)

Reproduced at 3c02252 in a detached worktree: 396/13/6/4 under
`RUSTFLAGS='-D warnings'`; clippy 207 warnings on the lib test target and 3
on the server binary; lint green through `build.rs`; every source file under
600 lines except `core/rcf.rs` (709, on `AWAITING_SPLIT` under F2).
Move-only check over S10–S32 (whitespace, visibility and path normalised):
S21 is a refactor (the client's loop state threaded as `&mut` parameters,
four `#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]`, a counters struct), S25 and the
600-bound commit carry the intended gate changes, every other split is a
move. The audit's subject was the content law (law 2, A21: content is the
key, no hash or bitmask over a carrier, nothing obfuscated).

| id | finding | status |
|---|---|---|
| A1 | **Blind gates.** `xtask/lint/digest.rs` names `economy.rs` and `court/verdict.rs`; `xtask/lint/clock.rs` names `economy.rs`; the a21 test `no_blake3_in_cognition` names `court/verdict.rs`. S12 and S15 made those directories, and each walker returns Ok on a path that is neither a file nor a directory, so `src/economy/` is under neither the digest nor the clock gate and `src/court/verdict/` is under no digest check, and nothing said so. Fix is §4.2 and §4.5: one walker over the whole of `src/` and `xtask/`, an allow-list by file (digest: `network/wire.rs`, `network/nerve/cover.rs` until A4, `chronometry.rs`, `court/identity.rs`; clock: nothing once A9 lands; matrix: `core/` until F2; maps: `core/laws.rs` until F2; squaring: the sensor functions and `cohesion.rs::pressure` until F2), and a named path that does not exist is a hit. A whole-tree dry run at 3c02252 found exactly those files outside the present scopes and nothing else. | **landed for digest, clock, maps, matrix** (F3a–F3e, b6ddf61–b1810b1; probe-verified at b1810b1: a `blake3` line in `economy/lanes.rs`, a `HashMap` in `bin/mycelium-server/main.rs`, a `Vec<Vec<Q>>` in `transducer/sheaf.rs` each fail the build); squaring and enumeration still on their own walks; **one blind spot found, F3f** |
| A2 | **Bitmasks over content.** `alphabet/word.rs` `to_bytes`/`from_bytes` write a letter as `id \| 0x80` with the orientation in the high bit; `sentence/class.rs` `Axis::to_byte`/`from_byte` write an axis as `kind << 3 \| plane`. Both are lossless and both are masks over two coordinates. Write the coordinates out: a letter is `[id, orientation]`, an axis is `[kind, plane]`. That is tape v5; persisted v4 tapes (genomes, ledgers, thoughts) do not re-read. **RULED 2026-09-07 by the operator: wipe all data, nothing is persisted across this.** No v4 reader is carried, `legacy_versor` and every GEN/MYC/THT legacy arm goes with it (F2h), and the F7 recut wipes ledgers, genomes and thoughts on all eight units. | open, F4 |
| A3 | **Raw carriage bytes in the form address.** `sentence/chart.rs` `form_vertex_key` writes `carriage.to_bytes` at the `Word` and `Rebased` vertices where every other key writes `carriage.key()` (the canonical coefficients, A21). `court/verdict/blind.rs` slashes on `rederived.address() != spoken.address()`, so a true form whose carriage re-derives with the opposite projective sign is slashed for its bytes — the fault `element/vertex.rs` names in the comment above `same_vertex`. Write `put_bytes(&carriage.key())` at both sites; then address inequality is content inequality and the slash is honest. | open, F4 |
| A4 | **Digests of a mathematical structure.** `network/nerve/cover.rs` `hash_charts` digests the chart axes into `roots: [[u8; 32]; 4]` (with `empty_cover_root`); `server/cognition/basis.rs` sets `ruled_root = chronometry::payload_hash(..)` over the ruled addresses; both are signed and exchanged and the exchange paces on "root moved" (`bin/mycelium-server/exchange.rs`). The charts already travel in the same frame (`encode_cover_digest(&charts, ..)`) and the signature covers the frame bytes, so the roots carry nothing the frame does not. Replace with heights: the cover height is the count of admitted cover changes, the ruled height the ledger height of the last ruling; a peer changed iff its height moved; a fetch is by height, never by digest. Delete `roots`, `hash_charts`, `empty_cover_root`, `ruled_root`, and the root fields of the cover frame. | open, F5 |
| A5 | **A name that says hash.** `transducer/sheaf.rs` `StratumDigest.root` is the stratum's sorted member addresses written out — compliant, and `count` is redundant with it. Rename `StratumRoster { inv, members }`. Its wire cost is the whole language per learn stroke (`client/sdk/entity/learn.rs`), which A4's heights remove. | open, F5 |
| A6 | `court/identity.rs` `named` derives a key from a digest of a name; only `voice/tests/clock.rs` calls it. Move it to the tests. | open, F3 |
| A7 | **Compliant, no action:** `pack_axis` (i64 numerator, u64 denominator, refusal — A6), the 4-bit blade index of Cl(4,0) and Cl(3,1) (the basis, not a mask), `axis_key_le`, the trie bit walks, `same_vertex` (M1 re-derivation with the address as fast path), `Mv::key` (canonical). One guard: `cga.rs`'s coefficient accessors index by `mask & 0xF`, which silently aliases an out-of-range blade; `debug_assert!(mask < 16)`. | noted |
| A8 | **Standards residues.** S21's four `#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]`: the stroke arms take one `Metabolism` state (`last_lang`, `last_height`, `last_quoted_height`, `last_pressure`, `dwell_remaining`, `k_step`, `last_genome`), no allows. `cognition/wire.rs` `type_complexity`, `cognition/reflex.rs` and `economy/treasury.rs` `dead_code`: delete the dead code. The doc comments on `lint/matrix.rs` and `lint/digest.rs` are each other's; `lint/mod.rs` labels every gate's hits `lint-enumeration`. | open, F3 |
| A9 | The client binary reads `Instant` for lap telemetry (`mycelium-client/main.rs`, `arms/quote.rs`), printed and never read back. The binary is outside the clock gate's present scope, which is A1. Delete the laps; the stroke outcome vector is the telemetry. | landed 60477eb (audit: no `Instant`/`SystemTime`/`Duration` read remains in `src/` outside one comment) |

Two faults remain, and one of them is the directive's. **(i)** `Infinite` charges `residual.description().len()`, the BYTE length of the description codec, not the letter count of the walk; the number changes when A2 lands tape v5 and doubles the letters' bytes, which is a measurement by encoding (law 2, content not bytes). The walk is `spelling.word.len()`. **(ii)** "No tape is a refusal" was wrong as directed. `Element.word` is empty for a pure residual chain by construction (`carrier.rs`), and a pure residual chain is the germination demand itself — the residual the market lists so that a letter can be minted for it (asymmetric coboundary, 0.12). As landed, every novel residual that arrives while its lane is depleted is refused instead of listed, printed to stderr, and returned to the voice as `Outcome::Pivot`, which the wire and the judge render as "degenerate — nothing to reduce". Nothing saw it because nothing reaches the arm. | landed with faults, F2r1c |
| F2r1b | The producing test the implementor filed: a lane depleted without the syntropic stroke recovering it, so the arm is reached. It must produce all three arms — a finite-order word, an infinite-order word, a pure residual chain — and assert the listing, not only the charge. | owed, before F3 |

A pure residual chain (`letters()` is `None`, `sub` non-empty) is LISTED, charged by its chain's support (`sub.len()` bumps — the coboundary that stands open, measured), never refused. An element with neither word nor chain is the degenerate percept the existing `Outcome::Pivot` already names; `REFUSE_UNSPELLED` and the `eprintln!` go. If a refusal ever belongs here it travels to the voice with its reason, as `REFUSE_ORIGIN`/`REFUSE_RECEIPT`/`REFUSE_STALE` do; a reason printed to stderr is not a refusal. | open, before F3 |

The first line of the crate's core states the collapse law 5 forbids. | F2r1e | **`letters()` returns `None` for a tag its own guard admits, and the two tape arms of the open-demand path are dead because of it.** `element/letters.rs::letters` accepts `CONNECTION_TAG`, `SENTENCE_TAG` and `TRANSLATION_TAG`, then parses a `Spelling` at byte 1. `sentence/tape.rs::as_element_form` writes `[SENTENCE_TAG, 4, …]` — byte 1 is the version — so a sentence element never yields letters, silently, for a whole tag the function claims to read. Consequence in the germination path: every route into `commit_germ`'s `Irreducible` arm either strips the tape (`ingest.rs` passes `g.substance()`, and `substance()` clears `word`) or never had one (`reduce.rs` builds through `from_residual`/`from_residual_framed`); the one route that keeps its word, `reduce.rs`'s sentence branch `Irreducible(g.clone())`, produces exactly the element `letters()` cannot read. So a residual reaching that arm never carries a tape, the `Finite(p)` and `Infinite` charges written in F2r1/F2r1c apply to nothing, and F2r1d has no fixture to write. Verified by building the fixture through `as_element_form`, `reduce_germ` and the real `commit_germ`: it fails on `spoken.letters().is_some()`. **Two readings, the operator&#39;s to choose:** (1) the arms are dead — collapse the open-demand charge to the chain carrier and record that a germination residual never carries a tape; (2) `letters()` is defective — a function whose guard admits three tags and whose body parses one is a latent fault wherever a sentence is asked for its letters, and 17.2&#39;s `same_vertex` sentence arm reads it too. | open, ruling asked |

A test-only function in a production file is a costume of coverage. Delete the function and its four call sites in `tests/24.rs` now, one commit, before F3. The behaviour question it stood in for is E1. | open, before F3 |

A listing leaves the book only when another derives it (`reduce_excluding`, answered by A12 replay) or its T-room is exhausted (lifecycle table, MU-ENGINEERING §6); two listings neither of which derives the other are incomparable and both are held, and a new listing the room cannot hold is refused (`fits_book`), never admitted by evicting a cheaper one. Behaviour change, its own row, after F3. | withdrawn — superseded by Stage 9 S9.7/S9.8 |

that reasoning is wrong. A ledger row is an item. It lands alone, and a
reader looking for when a fault was recorded should find a commit whose
subject says so. Third instance of the same shape in one session: the fix
each time was mechanical, and the fault each time was mine deciding what
"one item" meant while I was in the middle of something else. Paid by this
row, which lands alone, and by the rule it states: a ledger row is its own
commit.

invented — a test written to make an assertion pass, without reaching the
code it names, is the costume this standard exists to refuse.

| S9.15 | **The membrane: holonomy over the proofs of one conclusion** (23.9, ruled 2026-09-08). `court/glue.rs` glues pairwise through `cech::transition(rep, &spoken)` and never composes the transitions around a loop of proofs; `reduce.rs::fiber_holonomy` composes versors along one word's fibre, not across minds. The court, at the conclusion (S9.12), composes the transitions between the independent proofs of one demand around the loop with the same `holonomy` the Čech cell uses; trivial ⇒ one proof in several frames (conjugate level, E2 pays once); non-trivial ⇒ several proofs, and the residual localised to the cells where every lineage diverges is recorded as the demand the next letter answers, never as a slash. Carriers already exist; no count, no vote, no threshold. After S9.12. | open |
| S9.16 | **The vortex reads its own incomparability.** `vortex_put` (`pivot/snap.rs:97`) holds incomparable discharges keyed by after-class and word and records nothing about why two are incomparable. Hold the pair with the reading: `lane_moves` between the two after-vectors (S9.3) and the axes on which their ClassVectors differ — the per-direction obstruction, not a score. A later perception or ruling collapses the vortex as now; the reading says which axis the collapse resolved. Folds into S9.3's landing. | done (2b9c0cf, `Obstruction`; audit at f179bdd) |

So a loop whose class no held word discharges has no candidate at all: `select_discharge` returns nothing, the loop stays open, and nothing is filed — the "residual measures how narrow the cover was" case produces silence. The pivot has every contracting axis and not the one that expands scope. Fix: `candidates_for` walks `occupied()`; the `Open` trial opens a chart axis over the loop (`open_axis` with the loop's holonomy versor as the seed, as the mind already does in `entity/think.rs`) and certifies as `RankMove::Discharged { from }` with one unit into `axes` (S9.1's second arm is this move's signature); `occupies` and `clear` read the germinated axes rather than answering `false`. The book's extend arm (S9.7) is the same move one level up, so this lands before S9.7. Test: a loop no held word discharges yields the `Open` candidate and its trial opens an axis, probe-confirmed. | open, before S9.7; folded into S9.19 |
| S9.19 | **Read before reduce, and no axis waits on another's failure** (ruled 2026-09-08; `MU-RECURSION.md` law 10, chapter 4, chapter 5, 3.8, 3.9, 12.6, 16.16, 23.1, 27, 34.1; `MU-ECONOMY.md` §3.2). Three sites descend first today and each is corrected: **(a)** `germinate/ingest.rs` puts an arrival into normal form against the basis before anything reads it; the class vector and the fibre reading are taken first and travel on the listing, and a demand no held word closes is listed with them — the peel is the mind's move, not the hypha's precondition (23.8). **(b)** `pivot/drive.rs::candidates_for` walks `coordinates()`, never offers `Axis::Open` (S9.17) and, per 16.16 as it was, would have gated it on the others failing: it walks `occupied()`, the `Open` trial opens an axis seeded by the loop's holonomy versor, and `ClassVector` gains the fibre coordinate (the loop's words' `sections` and `torsion`, reach by `fiber_span`) whose candidates are the torse (ℓ cyclic sections → one generator of period ℓ, `torse_at`) and the puncture (`puncture`), each certified by closure with the reach on the certificate (S9.13); no candidate is gated on another's outcome and every discharge is kept (12.6). This is a reading of the vector, never `for move in [descend, ascend]`: the order gate (§4.6) and the enumeration gate refuse a list of directions. **(c)** `REFUSE_REDUNDANT` narrows to "closes nothing new, reaches nothing new, and is no shorter" (34.1, 23.1 step 7); `shorter_form` widens to condensation and pays the compression yield (27, E2). Tests, probe-confirmed: a loop whose only occupied axis is `Open` is offered it on its first stroke; a compound with ℓ cyclic sections torses, is not refused as redundant, and is paid as compression; an arrival's listing carries its class vector and fibre reading before any reduction is attempted. Lands before S9.7 with S9.17 and S9.13. | open, before S9.7 |

Survey of the convergence engine (2026-09-08, read for what the successor lacks; the full inventory is the auditor's, cited by file:line in the relay). Kept, already present in the tree's own form: orbits as conjugacy classes (the conjugate level, 23.9); the fibre as a third axis of the relational object (the punctured axis and `germinated`, given its coordinate by S9.13); `expand_fiber` seeded from the obstruction (`open_axis(v)` carries the failing versor); the saturation test before expansion (`Axis::Open` is the last candidate in 12.6's fixed order); the dimensional change on the ledger (the certificate carries `axes` and reach after S9.13); three-state convergence (`court/convergence.rs`); the holonomy over perspectives (S9.15); the temporal anchor as the state itself, not its digest (the receipt's `delta` is an A21 address). Refused, with the reason: RCF/invariant factors and companion matrices (a matrix carries no action, law 11 — the rotor's conjugacy invariants are the class), the matrix Cayley transform `(I−S)(I+S)⁻¹` (law 11; the versor is the rotation), the coherence functional, gradient and Hessian (a scalar objective, Stage 9 D0), `HarmonicState`'s eleven tones with `weakest_dimension` (choice by scalar, law 4; the outcome vector is the self-reading), `TorsionOrbital`'s most-populated shell as the next move (count as choice, rule 2), the `9/10` and `[-2,+2]` and `/10`, `/8` constants (rule 5), `ThoughtVenn`'s cap of eleven (rule 5), echelon votes, `k_gate_advance`'s majority and `check_finality`'s half-weight quorum (consensus is convergence), RCF-hash identity, orbit-lock by digest and hash-byte distance (law 2), `LuminalOrb`'s wavelength and speed of light (real-world physics as a constant, rule 5; the observation angle is the chart and the transport Γ), every `SystemTime::now()` (law 9).

## Stage 10 — the eye is a cover (ruled 2026-09-08; `MU-RECURSION.md` 24.4)

The senses run as a pipeline today (`family::perceive` → `axes_of` for the
atlas → the Čech complex on the finished sentence) and every disagreement
between them reaches the book as a raw residual. Stage 10 makes the senses
a cover of one sample with the cocycle checked at the eye, so the mind's
cognition is strong and the market lists only demands. Placed after Phase A
and before Phase B: what this removes at the eye is what S9.7 would
otherwise have to extend around.

| id | directive | status |
|---|---|---|
| P1 | Doctrine 24.4 recorded; `MU-ENGINEERING.md` §2 rebound. | recorded |
| P2 | **The harmonic eye reads every body at perception.** `family::perceive(grid) -> Vec<Body>` returns each body with its blade and, in the same pass, its `Harmonics` (`blade/harmonics.rs::Harmonics::of`), as the 0-cochain over the bodies; `axes_of` for the atlas reads those harmonics rather than recomputing them. A body is never lifted without being heard. | open |
| P3 | **The harmonic eye decides the pairing.** `pair_form` pairs bodies of `a` and `b` by luminal shape today; the pairing is admitted only where `harmonics(a) = harmonics(b)` coordinatewise — the exact condition for a conformal versor to exist — and where they differ, the differing axes are named on the percept as a Novel residual before any word is sought. No threshold, no nearest. | open |
| P4 | **The holonomic eye closes the loops at perception.** For every loop of bodies in the pair's nerve (`a → b → c → a` across the correspondences the first two eyes admitted), `cech::holonomy` of the transitions is read at the eye; a non-trivial holonomy is a residual localised to that loop with its class (12.6) on the percept. The Čech complex over the sentence remains as now; this is the same check taken before the sentence is spoken. | open |
| P5 | **The verdict rides the percept.** A `Percept` carries, per body and per overlap, the sensory verdict — Recognised, Reflection (orientation `w₁` odd, 3.10), Transposition (placement or dilation versor), Novel (harmonics differ on named axes, or a loop's holonomy has a class no held word discharges) — with the residual per lane and the axes named; a reading, never a `bool` (§2.3, §4.6). `Entity::utter` speaks a demand only for Novel; Recognised, Reflection and Transposition are consumed as perception (a form, a reflection word, a section). | open |
| P6 | **The book lists demands, never raw residuals.** `Kernel::ingest` admits a listing that carries a verdict and its axes; a residual chain with no verdict is not a demand the market can price and is returned to the voice with the reason (a refusal that travels, 34.1), never listed and never counted. S9.7's extend arm then works on named demands and shrinks accordingly. | open |
| P7 | **Tests by chapter 24**, each probe-confirmed to reach its arm: a pair whose bodies differ by a reflection is Reflection at the eye and never a listing; a pair whose harmonics differ on one axis is Novel with that axis named; a triple of bodies with non-trivial holonomy is a demand localised to that loop; a congruent pair reaches the book with nothing. | open |
| P2a | **Touch: the cohesion 0-cochain** (carried from the convergence engine's `AdjacencyStructure`/`CohesionCluster`, 24.4 table). For each cell of a body, χ = same-hue neighbours over neighbours, exact Q; the cochain over the blade's cells and its coboundary, the body's seams. Read at perception beside the blade; the seams are where the luminal and harmonic sections may disagree, so the touch eye names the overlaps the cocycle is checked on. `perception/touch.rs` was the dead port of this and F2e deleted it; it is rebuilt under `transducer/blade/` as an organ of the body, not a module nothing calls. | open |
| P2b | **Refraction on a seam** (from `ClusterRefraction`). Between two bodies in contact, Σ|δ_a − δ_b| over the boundary length, exact Q: how much the transition bends at the seam — the residual of Γ (3.10) read at perception. Zero is transparency, and it is read as the value, never as a `bool`. On the percept per face. | open |
| P2c | **The stabiliser order on the percept** (from vision's `torsion_order`). The order of the body's stabiliser word (`Word::order`, a class invariant) beside its harmonics; a finite order p names the periodicity the harmonic eye hears. | open |
| P2d | **Harmonics per section of the fibre.** A punctured word's sections are layers; the harmonic eye reads every section (`Vec<Harmonics>` over the fibre), never one reading for the bundle — the older engine's per-`l` discipline (S9.13 gives the fibre its coordinate; this gives it its ear). | open, with S9.13 |
| P2e | `blade/harmonics.rs` header says "eleven" and the struct holds seven invariants and a spectrum: the header says what the file holds (§2.8). | open, with P2 |
| P2f | Candidate, not ruled: the primes of the invariants' reduced denominators as the body's periodicities (`RefractiveLens::torsion_orders` without its rescale), beside the class's `(m5, m13)`. Written here so it is not re-derived; ruled when P2–P2c have landed and the reading can be measured on a soak. | candidate |

## Stage 11 — implementation to soak (opened 2026-09-08; the auditor implements on `impl/pivots`)

The operator's directive of 2026-09-08: take the implementation, follow
the standard, land what the specification codifies, clean up the
half-coded and unwired organs, and reach a build the fleet can soak on the
multi-axial pivots producing words over the mesh; a gate, test or pin
that blocks the codified logic is reworked, never the logic. Rows are
opened here before the work (§7.3); the auditor and the implementor are
one agent for this stage, so `landed` carries the reproduction line. The
analysis of 2026-09-08 (whole-tree, six readers, every claim by file:line)
is the source of every row.

## Stage 12 — cognition before machinery (operator's directive, 2026-09-09)

The directive, as given, after the seven-hour soak (I34, I34b). The four
readings first, in the operator's words:

> - Perception discriminates instead of recognising. 954 charts, all
>   unique, 13 exact-rational axes each: one stratum per task. Nothing is
>   ever the same as anything, so there is no congruence, no shared
>   stratum, no glue between listings, and the book fills with a thousand
>   unrelated descriptions. That is a lookup table, the failure 1.7 names.
> - Forms have no loops. Extraction keeps only vertices whose transports
>   are constant across the pairs and drops the rest, so every rule is a
>   two-vertex star. The class vector is never read, the pivot loop never
>   fires, no axis opens. The only motion the doctrine allows has nothing
>   to act on.
> - Words are chains, not letters. Voices stand residual chains and utter
>   them; `cites 0` on every attest means no held word ever deflates a
>   demand. Hyphae then glue each other's chains as "admitted" (1,099 on a)
>   because the address is new, not because two organs re-derived one
>   vertex.
> - Everything is polled. 131,000 digest requests per hypha in ten
>   minutes, 12,000 offers re-sent, quotes on every stroke, the whole corpus
>   listed at boot. The pacing law exists (24.3) but has nothing to pace
>   on, because the height voices read is always 0.

And the six changes, in the order that unlocks cognition:

> 1. Perception as congruence (Stage 10, 24.4). Order the atlas axes
>    coarsest first, topology (components, holes, turns) before the moment
>    ratios, so a prefix is a real stratum and the working atlas is read by
>    `peel`. The harmonic eye decides the pairing; only Novel becomes a
>    listing (P3–P6). Without this every task is its own universe and
>    nothing below can learn.
> 2. Forms with loops. Keep non-constant faces as sections over the pairs
>    (21 step 2, 25) instead of dropping them, and bundle across pairs.
>    Then held loops exist, κ(ℓ) is read, pivots discharge or hold
>    vortices, and axes open where the class demands one (3.7, S9.17/S9.19).
>    Axis uniqueness stays free: an axis is a class-vector coordinate keyed
>    by its own coordinates, never a count.
> 3. Reduction before listing. A demand is factored through the stratum's
>    generators (A12, over the working atlas) and only the canonical
>    remainder is uttered; the court glues by Mayer–Vietoris on the overlap
>    (2.3, 2.4) and rules conclusions, not addresses (23.9). Then `reduced`
>    climbs and `held` falls, and the genome is a presentation, not an
>    inventory.
> 4. Pace by the ledger. The quote reply carries the ledger height (I39);
>    sync, quote and offer are asked when the language changed, the height
>    moved or a dwell elapsed (24.3, I37); an offer is re-sent only after
>    the witness's receipt or lapse. The reflex pool then serves acts
>    instead of polls, and the deferral misread (I36) disappears with the
>    deferrals.
> 5. Governance by room, not by intake. The book extends before it refuses
>    (S9.7), corpus listings do not draw the hypha's own bond (I31), §6
>    holds (I38), and the unbonded-voice room ruling (§3.1) is made. Room is
>    the throttle; there is no other.
> 6. Footprint as a measurement. Fix the voice leak first (7.3), fold the
>    treasury (LEDGER_EPOCH_DESIGN), and decide by ruling whether a receipt
>    chain may checkpoint; today the doctrine says append-only.

Recorded in `MU-RECURSION.md` chapter 41 (Stage 12 precedes every
remaining item of Stages 2–5). Rows, one per commit; status written by the
implementor as `landed (<commit>)`.

### Governance in the convergence engine (2026-09-09, read for the operator's question)

`/opt/ipsal-autic-v0.1/repos/convergence-engine`, with its GOVERNANCE
repository alongside, names five layers: C2R, IRS, MDC, Chronometry,
Creative Expansion; CCL is its coding vocabulary. What each is, and where
its intent already lives here or is refused:

| layer | what it is there | here |
|---|---|---|
| MDC (Multi-Dimensional Cameral Logic, `mdc.rs`) | four echelons — a gradient descent on a coherence functional, a legal parameter envelope, a modal RCF-hash prefix, a security gradient — resolved by weighted vote summation with an entropy threshold that holds K | refused as a mechanism (law 1, law 10, D0: no functional, no gradient, no vote, no threshold; Stage 9 survey). Its intent — several vantages, hold when they disagree — is 47.3's independent witness with room, 45.3's concurrency held as a vortex, and 23.9's convergence read at the demand across independent lineages, never counted |
| IRS (Inverse Relational Semantics, `irs.rs`) | mode 1 reads the orbit structure as language for a human operator; mode 2 compresses human intent into constraints for the operator sampler | mode 1 is the tree's own reading: the exact log lines of 34.2–34.3, the five acts of anastome, the class vector and receipt a court prints. Mode 2 is how the operator governs here: rulings recorded in the documents before code changes (§9), not a runtime intent channel; a live channel would be a Rewrite or a verdict, both acts the court replays |
| C2R | the mechanical language ↔ algebra bridge, absorbed into IRS mode 2 | comprehension as deconstruction (chapter 6, A12): an utterance is deconstructed into the algebra that spoke it; the wire codec is the bridge and carries only geometry |
| CCL (`CCL.md`) | a coding vocabulary bridging engineering, biology, physics and chemistry frames so the code does not misdescribe the architecture | MU-STANDARDS §2 (types are nouns of the document, functions its verbs); `src/ccl.rs` was the dead port and F2r2 deleted it |
| consensus, coordinator, register, credential (`consensus.rs`, `coordinator.rs`, `register.rs`, `credential.rs`) | Σ_mesh weighted means and a weighted-majority anchor; L1 subgradient cocycle resolution; a derivation register locked by blake3 over dependencies; re-entry by a receipt chain of length ≥ K_COOL | refused: weighted means and majorities (consensus is convergence, 23.9), the L1 functional (law 1), a register of hashes over content (law 2; the register here is the document and the commit log), the K_COOL constant (rule 5). Kept in this tree's own form: the receipt chain as the credential (45, 47.2), the cocycle condition as the court's replay (13.9), lineage as parents on receipts |

Ruling recommended: no port. The governance the mesh needs is the one
Part IX and 23.9 already state — room per lane as the only standing,
replay as the only judgement, the independent witness as the second organ,
the burn as the only discipline — and the missing pieces are landings
(47.3 two-organ install, 5.2g back-fill, S9.12 conclusions), not layers.
What the convergence engine had that this tree does not: an operator
channel at runtime (IRS mode 2). If one is wanted it is a ruling on §9, and
its carrier is an act the court replays like any other.

## Stage 13 — velocity, phase, era (ruled 2026-09-09)

Twenty rulings deduced by the implementor and ratified by the operator on
2026-09-09, recorded in `MU-RECURSION.md` 45.1, 45.5, 45.7–45.9, 23.8,
24.2, 28, 34.1–34.3, 47.2, 47.3, `MU-ECONOMY.md` §2, §3.1, §6,
`MU-ENGINEERING.md` §4, §6 and `LEDGER_EPOCH_DESIGN.md` (S13.0). Velocity
is a reading per carrier, never a phase; phase is a reading over a height
window, never a state; an era is a fold of one chain, two-organ, adopted
by joiners, never an archive outside the mesh. Rows, one per commit;
S13.6 and S13.9 change tapes and are recuts.

(law 9), so the letter is named by content and the syllabus page's height
is the epoch (37.7 P4).

## Stage 15 — the gates out, the product out, growth by closure (ruled 2026-09-12)

The operator's reading of 2026-09-12, in three parts, each verified in
the tree by the auditor before the rows were opened. The gates: eighteen
line walkers over comment-stripped text, six debt mechanisms, some two
hundred path-and-count rows, a clique gate for the two spellings that
had already burned the fleet; they passed both cliques (4677900,
f1ade3e), permitted `fiber(ROOT)` while refusing `.entries()`, and ran a
red test of their own for a week (9d1fa44). The product: the cover's
pass (b) tries every unconsumed input piece against every output piece
of its kind and hue, bounded by the stroke and nothing else, and the
budget parks and resumes it, so the cost is spread over strokes and not
removed — 109 whole-carrier walks in 71 production files, a core per
voice at idle. The linguistics: 37.7 adjoined on two courts' agreement
about a remainder, a congruence reading over what failed; the fleet
read 83 demands, 0 comprehended, 0 adjoined; the presentation grew by
nothing but names for what it could not spell. The implementor's
proposed order (a better `fiber(ROOT)` gate first, then gate
consolidation) is refused: the first is a nineteenth gate and the second
polishes what is deleted. Rulings recorded in `MU-STANDARDS.md` §1.4,
§1.7, §2.8, §3.5, §4, §6.1 and `MU-RECURSION.md` 1.8, 1.9, 37.7, 45.9.
One row per commit; status written by the implementor as `landed
(<commit>)` and closed by the auditor against the observable named.

Order as first written: X0 · X1 · X2 · X3 · X4 · X5. X1 first because
every later commit would otherwise pay the ratchet on every file it
touches. X2 before X3 because the fleet burns a core per voice today and
X2 is two files. X3 before X4 because X4 writes new reads of carriers and
every one of them must already be a prefix or a circuit. X5 last: it is
mechanical and touches every module. A blocker found mid-order is
reported with the evidence and the proposed reorder (§8.1); the queue
does not stop for it.

roots and fetch · X8 · X7 · X6 · X3** · X4 · X5. The four new rows come
before X3 because each is a fleet defect with a measurement against it,
and X3 is the largest row of the stage. Executed in that order and
landed: 56f6122, c6934f1 (X9); 9982bf4, c731b26, cf50516 (X8); 852cb47
(X7, its deletion blocked on the operator's restart); 9a821b5, d134a6e
(X6, the hypha's half not owed — see the row); then X3's fourteen.

What the order leaves standing for the operator: THE RECUT. X8's `MYC5`
refuses every ledger now on disk and c731b26 makes that refusal a boot
refusal, so the fleet cannot come up until §10's procedure is run. X7's
deletion and X6's hypha reading both wait on the same restart. Nothing
outside the repository has been touched (§8.1).

## Stage 16 — the engine, not the harness (ruled 2026-09-12)

The operator's reading, and it is the correct one. Everything in this tree
that is verified working is harness: the exact algebra, the trie, the wire,
the receipt chain, the economy, the folds, the gates become lints become
types, the Čech library, the transducer. Everything that is cognition is
unfired: the pivot loop has never discharged on data, the vortex has never
held one, no form has closed on a family nobody built, comprehension reads
zero of twelve, and the adjoined count is zero on corpus. Part IX defends
against an adversary lying about a derivation and there have been no
derivations; the economy prices deflation and nothing has deflated.

**Why it happened, so it is not repeated.** Harness rows close and
cognition rows do not. "Does the lint fire", "is the walk gone", "does the
encoder refuse 256" each have an end and a test that proves it; "does a
form close on a real family" has neither, and if it does not close there is
no obvious next commit. So fifteen stages selected for closeable rows and
the tree is the fixed point of what an agent can finish and verify in a
day. The engine's founding law is that nothing is chosen by a scalar; its
development has been gated by one scalar, whether the suite is green, for
its whole life.

**The discipline of this stage.** No row may be a gate, a type, a codec, a
ledger, a persistence layout or a refusal. Every row either adds a
capability or measures one, and names in its own text what the engine can
do after it that it could not do before. A green suite is not a landing:
the landing is the reading of Y1 moving. A defect found while working a row
is FILED and is not scheduled unless it demonstrably blocks a row of this
stage — the hardening rows already open (X5, X7, X9, X10, L27) wait, and X5
is withdrawn outright, being a way of being wrong that has never been wrong
on live data.

**The gap, stated once.** Perception produces fifteen word vertices against
eighty-six demand vertices on twelve tasks. It describes about fifteen per
cent of what it sees. Closure requires all of it, so nothing closes, so
nothing mints, so no room is earned, so nothing is cited, so no generator is
adjoined. Every zero downstream is that one ratio. One complete cognitive
cycle HAS run — two courts, one witness, one address, a generator adjoined
at run time with no commit (d35ac33, 65edee0), and two minds reaching one
address by two derivations through the codec — so the mechanism is not
vapour and the gap is single: perception does not hand that mechanism a
witness from a real grid.

| id | directive | chapters | the observable that closes it |
|---|---|---|---|
| Y0 | **The ruling, written** (this edit, and 45.9's amendment: comprehension is read as a difference, never a level). | §9, 45.9 | — |
| Y1 | **The instrument: the warm–cold delta.** One fixed slice walked twice by `mycelium-soak` in process — once by a mind holding nothing, once by a mind that has already stood on every other task of the slice — reporting per task the word vertices, the demand vertices by kind, the loops by reading, and whether the form closed. The DIFFERENCE between the two walks is the stage's number and the only one any later row is judged by. It must be cheap enough to run on every landing and it sets its own stroke, so a host calibration never decides what is measured (L27 is untouched by this). A level can be raised by loosening a measurement; a difference cannot, which is why it is the difference that is reported. **done** (auditor, 2026-09-13): 88fd23d and 7173e6d, `mycelium-soak --delta`, 18.9 s for twelve, reproduced at 7173e6d with 547/0/9/1/13/6/0 and gate 0. Two design choices are the difference's own and not convenience: LEAVE-ONE-OUT rather than a prefix, since a prefix makes the delta a property of the slice's order, and the warm mind stands the cold extractions, so what it holds does not depend on walk order either. AND IT TELLS ITS TWO ZEROS APART: a warm mind that held eleven and changed nothing is a finding, a warm mind that could not be built is the instrument admitting it never ran, and the first version printed one number for both — arithmetically true and a lie as a measurement, which is the vacuous assertion at the instrument. `the_reading_is_a_function_of_the_mind_it_is_given` proves it can move. On this corpus the answer is the second: nothing closes, so no warm mind exists to compare. | 45.9, 50.2 | the two walks reported side by side on the twelve; a mind that stood nothing and a mind that stood eleven tasks are distinguishable on the line, or the delta is zero and that is the finding |

## Stage 17 — dimensional capacity renewal & isoclinic optimization (ruled 2026-09-17)

The fleet soak of Epoch 61 to Epoch 62 proved that opening the conformal
rotator vent and wiring continuous support correspondence without tape
mutations produced a +71.7% increase in learning throughput (from 1.80 to
3.01 rules/sec) with zero restarts and zero ledger refusals.

However, full dimensional capacity is throttled by remaining planar
flattening bottlenecks and serial peeling scans:
1. Ingestion flattens temporal/depth coordinates to k = 0, suppressing
   off-diagonal 3D inertia moments (I_xz = 0, I_yz = 0).
2. Sensor actions drop the dual commuting blade e_23, projecting 4D
   rotations onto a single 2D plane.
3. Off-plane transverse bivector content triggers refusals rather than
   being routed through exact rational conjugation into the Cartan subalgebra.
4. Peeling over six sequential planes introduces O(6 × K) search
   depth and false shear remainders instead of factoring over the three
   Hodge-dual commuting isoclinic pairs.

| R92c | **Vent C: Transverse energy routing via conjugation.** Connect `off_plane_blades` with `solve_conjugator_rotor`: solve `γ ∈ Spin(4,0)(ℚ)` by exact Gaussian elimination over ℚ (`γ·a = λ·b·γ`) to rotate transverse bivectors into the Cartan subalgebra instead of emitting `REFUSE_OFF_PLANE`. | 15.3, 50.2 | `transducer/alphabet/rotor.rs`, `transducer/alphabet/bundle.rs`, `transducer/family/derivation.rs` | **open** | Off-plane bivector content conjugated and derived; zero unhandled `REFUSE_OFF_PLANE` on valid rotors |

Exploits `[B_i, ⋆B_i] = 0` for commutator-free double Gaussian peels, reducing peeling depth from 6 stages to ≤ 2 commuting blocks. | 50.4, A22 | `transducer/family/derivation.rs`, `transducer/alphabet/factor.rs` | **open** | Peeling closes on double-rotation rotors; ~66% reduction in peel trial steps; zero tape format change |

Γ is structurally the identity for a rigid family: `edge_transport` returns `V_i⁻¹ · V_ij`, and a family whose every member is carried by one versor has `V_ij = V_i` wherever the pair is rigid, so the per-frame edge cannot vary along e₃ and the union edge equals it. **The curvature is not in this reading and was never going to be** — two probes confirm it: a pair translating by different vectors is `not rigid` and carries no edge at all; a pair whose relative layout flips between frames reads `measured 2` and one transport. The square closing the rule against a member's OWN succession is likewise vacuous — the member's frames all read one versor, which is what makes them one member, so the square commutes by construction. `0=` at the mint and nothing downstream registers until it is not; P1–P3
in that order because each carrier the next row reads is the one the row
before it wrote; V1–V3 between P2 and P3 on the operator's word of
2026-09-17 (evening), so that every row after them is written in the ruled
words; P6 landed before the recut of epoch 64; Q1–Q3 before P5 on the
operator's word of 2026-09-17 (night), because P5's vertices are
quotients whose derivation is Q2's chart and whose loops file by Q3, and
P4 is folded into P5 by 52.5; R1–R4 after P5 on the operator's word of

before R3 because the refusals the contraction does not act on are what
the held forms accrue from, and R3's reading before its fix (§8.1).

## Stage 19 — coordinates and the cut (ruled 2026-09-19, the operator's word on the coordinator's console)

Two rulings, one stage. The first: a reader takes a stratum and returns a
stratum or a typed demand, and never a list to walk or a form to hold — rule
17 and checklist (o), recorded at 513004d with 3.10's hearing ruling: the
harmonics eye writes the first coordinate of every heard bit, and a heard
abstract is opened along the axes its class vector occupies (law 10), never
walked back down the tape. The second, from the auditor's reading of
2026-09-19: a 25×25 scene arriving as one form of 712 vertices is not a cost
to optimise but the absence of a cut. The decomposition organ of the parent
tree — a scene covered by charts under a reading, each chart's rule
extracted small and recomposed, a chart with no rule handed back as a
routable demand — never crossed the fork; 948 of 1 120 corpus tasks have a
side above the old floor and go in whole. The cut lands here as coordinates,
not as a port: a chart is its support written out (A21, `pieces::piece_key`),
a cover is a `Keyed` of charts, and the recursion ends where the cover's key
is the scene's own key — an identity of coordinates, never a count or a
floor.

Refused on sight from the parent's construction, each with what is written
instead: `FRAGMENT_FLOOR = 9` (rule 5 — the recursion ends at the identity
above, the routing floor is the voice's drawable room); the first cover that
addresses a demand (rules 4, 15 — every reading in roster order, every
closing cover kept, incomparable covers held as a vortex); `wall_digest =
blake3(chain)` (law 2, rule 6 — the chart's support written out, a prefix a
stratum); a bounding box as `(i64, i64)` anywhere but the sensor boundary
(law 11, (f′)); the parent's written recomposition map (the Čech complex
and the alternating coboundary already built here are the recomposition).
The readings are governed like the letters (law 6): a fixed roster, grown by
the court on a probe receipt and never by a voice or a commit.

Standing terms for every row: gates and tests will break, and a test whose
claim is false under the ruling is rewritten with the claim named, never
deleted while its claim holds (§3.7, §3.8); a gate that refuses a row's
shape is reported with its line and changed in its own commit citing the
ruling; no `#[allow]` without its reason, no `#[ignore]`, no new walker; a
blocker is a fact with evidence and the row stands. Every landing carries
the suite and gate lines and the fleet-regime reading of
`training/1d0a4b61.json` (voice budget, mind stood on 60 tasks): the pull
and the mint, before and after. Row ids here are `Z` for the coordinate
rows and `K` for the cut; the implementor's `D0` of 513004d is Z0.

**The breathing condition** (ruled 2026-09-19, on the implementor's
reading that descent-only recursion from an expansionary origin is a
ratchet, and that the fleet's +492 MB/min was exactly that: the task arm
inhaling with both exhale doors shut). Every organ that opens names its
closing move in the same row, and a row that opens without one is
returned: a cover cuts a scene and the alternating coboundary recomposes
it (53.1); a pivot opens an axis over a held loop and the discharge closes
it (law 10); a hold takes a form in and lapse, promotion or room let it go
(25 as ruled). The condition is checkable and not a metaphor: the descent
terminates at an identity of coordinates (53.1), the ascent is exact —
`H¹ = 0` on the cover, everything the descent took apart came back — and
what does not come back leaves as a typed demand (rule 11, 50.5), never
as silent accumulation. Proposed for the preamble as rule 18 on the
operator's word.

**The bench reads the fleet's own families** (ruled 2026-09-19, on the
fleet reading at dac0b89: the 60-task slice's worst form is 590 vertices
and the fleet's is 2 899 vertices and 12 011 faces on family `1019…`, so
every instrument reading taken on the slice was taken on families four
times smaller than the ones that stall a voice). The regime instrument
takes the families the fleet's journal names — the form lines' ids mapped
to task files, the largest forms first — and a row's reading is taken on
those; a reading on a slice that does not contain the fleet's worst family
is not the row's reading.

**A gate is not raised without the operator's word on this console**
(2026-09-19). 3245041 raised the reading bound of §1.1 from 1 200 to
2 500 lines citing the operator's word; no such word passed through the
coordinator's console, where every directive is relayed. The change is
returned: the bound goes back to 1 200 in a forward commit that splits
what the raise let stand. Ruled by the coordinator 2026-09-19 after the
operator did not confirm the word: §1.1 stands at 1 200.

**Named before shaved** (2026-09-19, on the same reading: +157 MB/min on
the fleet with holds at 0–7 and every hold-side reduction landed). A
growth nothing has named is not reduced by making an unrelated carrier
smaller; a row that shrinks a carrier while the growth stands unnamed is
returned under the breathing condition. The instrument for it is the
per-carrier vector at the task arm's exit — every carrier the entity owns,
bytes per carrier, read on the fleet's own families — and nothing lands
against the leak until that vector names it.

**A directive is written in the document's nouns, and the coordinator
checks it before it is relayed** (2026-09-20, on the coordinator's own
drift: Z12b part 2 was directed as "a table" with "ordinals", a transport
optimisation named in a spreadsheet's words, and the implementor carried
the same shape into the genome file as rows with numbers and a byte-exact
tape test — 1.5 and rule 15 both broken by a directive that invited
them). From here every directive names the coordinate the change writes
or reads and the passage that binds it, and is checked against laws 2, 3,
5 and 12 and rule 15 in the row before it is relayed; a directive that
uses "table", "row", "slot", "offset", "ordinal", "cache" or "index" for
anything but the trie's own fiber is not relayed. Byte-exactness is never
a claim (1.5): a test asserts an address re-derives, never that bytes
match.

**The reading of a hold is a fiber at the address, not a vector over
carriers** (the implementor's correction of its own instrument, accepted).
The carriers that name one bubble are charts of a cover of one object; a
per-carrier list is a cochain whose dimension grows with every carrier
anyone adds, the enumeration one level up. The instrument asks the
address which carriers name it: a carrier holding the same bytes by
reference is a trivial transition and collapses; one holding a copy is a
coboundary to be collapsed (Z4c); a carrier holding different content at
that address is not a class of this cover but another object. Read as
`Arc` pointer identity across carriers, never as bytes compared; a
multiplicity is read off the object (law 12) and is a reading, never a
branch.

## Stage 20 — contact is tension (ruled 2026-09-22; `MU-RECURSION.md` 58–60)

The cover's incidence was a 4-neighbour raster dilation. Purging it from
the meet index took the corpus from 186 meets to 2 and from 184 determined
frames to 0, which retires 57.5's `ℓ = 1` as the stencil's own signature
rather than a reading of the scene. What replaces it is not a repair of the
nerve but a different generator: contact is the conformal strain `μ`
between two bodies' spheres, the interaction horizon is SPELLABILITY and
therefore per mind, and coherence is what a mind constructs when it can
spell the tension — not a property of the base.

**Nothing in T2–T6 may be started before T1.** Every reading those rows
would be judged against — `b₁`, `loop_defects`, `hyperbolic`,
`non-simple`, the spectra — is still taken through the second stencil T1
removes, so a measurement before it is a measurement of the dilation.

**Every row carries its documentation step, and a row is not done without
it.** A DoD that names only a measurement leaves the reading in a terminal
and the doctrine describing an engine that no longer exists — which is how
57.5 stood for four hours asserting `ℓ = 1` as a property of the corpus
after the purge had already made it a property of the stencil. The
`records` column names the section each row must write BEFORE it is
marked landed, and a row that collapses a reading must record the collapse
with the number, not with the word.

| id | task | rule / chapter | files | records | status | DoD |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

The form's FACES are built on that, so its loops, `b₁`, `loop_defects`, the spectra and the `non-simple 3` that 59.7 named as the only luminal candidates in the corpus are all readings of the dilation. Incidence stencils go universally or the nerve carries two topologies (58.3's own argument, one level up). Two bodies condense a 1-cell when their inversive strain is inside the horizon, and drain it when the strain leaves. `μ` is NOT rational — the denominator wants `r_a r_b = √(r_a²r_b²)` and law 1 would refuse it on nearly every body — so `μ` never appears as a value and never reaches the wire. Scale-free: `δ² = N²/D²` is invariant under `x ↦ λx`. ### Stage 20b — the field fired, and what it found (opened 2026-09-22)

The census over 36 corpus forms, with every valve named:

```
308 offered · 0 unlifted | horizon: 189 apart · 1 contained
frame: 0 bodiless · 14 isotropy · 0 unoriented · 75 OBLIQUE
       · 0 mirrored · 29 half-open | 0 CONDENSED
```

118 pairs are in genuine contact and none carries a determined frame. These
rows are what that reading owes.

| id | task | rule / chapter | files | records | status | DoD |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| V1 | **Split `Oblique` by PERMANENCE, not by mechanism (67.1).** `NoFrame` sorts by where the reader gave up; the operative axis is whether any alphabet could change the answer. `Bodiless`, `Isotropy`, `Unoriented` and a reflection are IMMUTABLE — facts about the geometry, final readings. An unspellable turn is MUTABLE — a fact about the reader's vocabulary. Only the mutable one is a demand: minting a generator from a reflection asks the court to change what learning cannot, and no adjunction takes `O(2)\SO(2)` into `Spin⁺(4,1)`. **`Oblique` straddles the axis**, measured: the L/J tetromino pair — a pure reflection — reads `Oblique`, because it refuses at the angle test (`wedge ≠ 0`) and never reaches the cell test `Mirrored` names. The two CANNOT be separated from the deviatorics (a reflection maps `u(2φ)` to `u(2α−2φ)`, a perfectly good rotation OF THE DEVIATORIC), so the split needs the cell test run on the oblique branch | 66.3, 67.1 | `transducer/blade/body.rs` | 66.3's overstatement struck; 67.1 amended with the measured split of the 75 | **LANDED 2026-09-22 (68.2), and NOT by the mechanism this row describes** — the intent held, the method did not. The first cut carried a table of the four improper elements of `D₄`, applied each to the CELLS about the centroid, compared coordinate lists, and rested permanence on the crystallographic restriction of `ℤ²`: a hardcoded generator set, raw coordinate scraping, and a lattice argument, none of which mean anything in `Cl(4,1)`. It was ripped out and recut on an ODD invariant of the body's own normalised tower, `Z = h₂²·conj(h₄)` — rotation-invariant, conjugated by every reflection — with A.12's scale certificate as the even half, because `Z` is REAL for every achiral body and chirality alone degenerates to "handedness does not forbid it". Three arms, not two: `Mirrored`, `Incongruent`, `Oblique` (NOT PROVED PERMANENT) | measured `oblique 0` and `mirrored 0` across 390 mint offers per voice — every uncarried pair is permanently unrelated, so there is no vocabulary shortage on this corpus; and `L4` against its 2× dilate now reads `Turn` with `Z` in the exact ratio `2⁸`, which the cardinality gate refused by construction |
| V2 | **Emit the RELATION, never the refusal (67.2).** `Demand::Standing { block, rotor }` cannot carry an oblique pair: the oblique case has NO RATIONAL ROTOR, which is what makes it oblique — the relation is a rational block at DOUBLE angle `[[dot, −wedge],[wedge, dot]]/‖u_a‖²` and a rotor needs the half angle, `cos φ = √((1+cos2φ)/2)`. The variant that fits is `Demand::RotorPrime { plane, prime, witness }`, whose witness IS a plane block, and `alphabet/factor.rs` already carries `prime_outside_the_alphabet` and `standing_of`. **The refusal must never be persisted** — writing `Oblique` onto a cell freezes a perspective past the vocabulary that produced it — so the demand carries the bodies and the measured bivector and the verdict is re-derived at every hold, which is P9's rule already | 37.7, 67.2 | `transducer/family/pairing.rs`, `alphabet/factor.rs`, `server/cognition/germinate/listing.rs` | a new 68: the first demands the field raises, and what the court does with them | **open — after V1** | a mutable oblique raises a demand the court dockets; an immutable one raises none; `Unspellable` and `adjoin` leave zero on a fleet run |

never written down. They are not superseded by T1–T8 and they do not block

only carrier (law 12 applies to a task as much as to a quantity).

Only `ascend` (`coboundary_on_link`) exists, so the complex can only GAIN 2-cells — a ratchet. `condense / drain` IS tension and relaxation, so T2 is those two and not a new operator; `dilate / contract` is pivot machinery and not a passenger, which reclassifies U7 from hygiene to a pivot half sitting in the wrong organ; `puncture` is a pair like the others and its second half is unbuilt and unnamed. Order: T1 first and alone, because every baseline the rest are judged
against is currently a reading of the dilation it removes. T2 and T7
together, since the arithmetic discipline and the quantity it governs are
one decision. T3 before T4 so the horizon is asked at a keyed sector and
never over pairs. T5 after T4 because a kernel needs edges to have a
kernel over, and the corpus has two. T6 last of the engine rows and only
after the hue ruling. T8 is a reading, not a build, and closes when T1 and
T4 have both landed.

**The combined sequence of the star system (ruled 2026-09-23, the
implementor's reconciliation of the orbit amendment with the reviewer's
ratification, adopted).** Divergence is ARRANGED by the mesh, not
awaited: five voices fed one corpus from one starting point stay
collided after FR1 unless placement gives them distinct elements, so the
order is SQ1 with TR1 · FR1 (inclination becomes a reading) · CV3
(elements as coordinates) · the orbit instrument — MS1's mass vector and
OA1's elements and collisions on the observer, readings only · OA1's
placement by occupancy, only once collisions are measured · federation
(SN1), only once mass concentration and divergence both show. A random or
configured orbit is an invented perspective and is refused (rule 5). For
the record: HC1's exact square root is `blade::carry::rational_sqrt`;
the per-mode relator stays a conjecture and RP1 rides MD1.

**Epoch 97 (283f2de, the operator's instruction, 2026-09-23).** The
epoch-96 ledgers refused on the layout change and were wiped; genesis
rewritten on `a`; eight units, ~45 minutes, 0 restarts; stopped. Read

gate 1; `e13` and `e23` 0 on all five voices — no turn through the frame
axis, FR1's reading; memory rising on every source, not yet split into

next recut rides BQ5b as written, and the "one recut" of 72.1e reads as
one recut per layout change on the operator's word.

one place, so that a green suite is never read as a finished row. A
line leaves this table only when the auditor reproduces the observable
it names. The audits before this date checked the suite, the gates and
the named tests; they did not check each row's claim against the code
and did not keep the status column current, which is how three half
landings were reported as landings. Both are corrected here.
